Please generate JML specifications for the Java program given below.
```
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```

public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

Please generate JML specifications for the Java program given below.

public class RotateMatrix180 {
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}

```
Rules (apply all, exactly as written):
- DO NOT modify the original source code. Only JML specifications are allowed to be modified.
- Every method must have at least one postcondition ("ensures" clause). Add more if needed to strengthen the specification.
- Every method must have a frame condition ("assigns" clause). Specify the exact frame condition or use "assigns \nothing" if no memory location is modified.
- Every loop must have loop invariants ("maintaining" clauses).
- Do NOT use assumptions ("assume" clause).
- Do NOT specify exceptional behavior ("signals" clauses). Exclude all exception-causing states via preconditions ("requires" clause).
- If the method returns a newly allocated object, state the freshness with "ensures \fresh(\result);".
- Provide a STRONG and COMPLETE specification: cover all functional behavior, state effects, and return value. The specification must contain enough detail to allow to reconstruct the original code unambiguously.


assistant: ```
public class RotateMatrix180 {
    /*@ 
      @ // Behavior: A is null or empty -> return null
      @ behavior null_input:
      @   requires A == null || A.length == 0;
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result == null;
      @ 
      @ // Behavior: first row is null or empty -> return null
      @ also
      @ behavior bad_first_row:
      @   requires A != null && A.length > 0 && (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0);
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result == null;
      @ 
      @ // Behavior: some row (1..m-1) is null or has different length -> return null
      @ also
      @ behavior ragged_or_null_row:
      @   requires A != null && A.length > 0 && A[0] != null && A[0].length > 0
      @            && (\exists int k; 1 <= k && k < A.length; A[k] == null || A[k].length != A[0].length);
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result == null;
      @ 
      @ // Behavior: well-formed rectangular non-empty matrix -> return fresh rotated matrix
      @ also
      @ behavior ok:
      @   requires A != null && A.length > 0 && A[0] != null && A[0].length > 0
      @            && (\forall int k; 1 <= k && k < A.length; A[k] != null && A[k].length == A[0].length);
      @   // method allocates and writes the returned matrix and its subarrays
      @   assigns \result, \result[*], \result[*][*];
      @   ensures \result != null;
      @   ensures \fresh(\result);
      @   // dimensions preserved
      @   ensures \result.length == A.length;
      @   ensures (\forall int r; 0 <= r && r < \result.length; \result[r] != null && \result[r].length == A[0].length);
      @   // returned matrix is the 180-degree rotation of A
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length;
      @              \forall int j; 0 <= j && j < A[0].length;
      @                \result[i][j] == A[A.length-1-i][A[0].length-1-j]);
      @   // original matrix not modified
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length;
      @              \forall int j; 0 <= j && j < A[0].length;
      @                A[i][j] == \old(A[i][j]));
      @ 
      @ // Behaviors cover all cases and are disjoint
      @ complete behaviors;
      @ disjoint behaviors;
      @*/
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        /*@ 
          @ // check remaining rows are non-null and of length n
          @ maintaining 1 <= i && i <= m;
          @ maintaining m == A.length && n == A[0].length;
          @ maintaining (\forall int k; 1 <= k && k < i; A[k] != null && A[k].length == n);
          @ loop_writes i;
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        /*@
          @ // outer loop: rows 0..i-1 of ret have been filled correctly
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @ maintaining (\forall int p; 0 <= p && p < i;
          @                 \forall int q; 0 <= q && q < n;
          @                   ret[p][q] == A[m-1-p][n-1-q]);
          @ loop_writes ret[*], i;
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            /*@
              @ // inner loop: columns 0..j-1 of ret[i] have been filled correctly;
              @ // previous rows remain correct
              @ maintaining 0 <= j && j <= n;
              @ maintaining (\forall int p; 0 <= p && p < i;
              @                 \forall int q; 0 <= q && q < n;
              @                   ret[p][q] == A[m-1-p][n-1-q]);
              @ maintaining (\forall int q; 0 <= q && q < j;
              @                 ret[i][q] == A[m-1-i][n-1-q]);
              @ loop_writes ret[i][*], j;
              @ decreases n - j;
              @*/
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}
```

      @                A[i][j] == \old(A[i][j]));
      @ 
      @ // Behaviors cover all cases and are disjoint
      @ complete behaviors;
      @ disjoint behaviors;
      @*/
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        /*@ 
          @ // check remaining rows are non-null and of length n
          @ maintaining 1 <= i && i <= m;
          @ maintaining m == A.length && n == A[0].length;
          @ maintaining (\forall int k; 1 <= k && k < i; A[k] != null && A[k].length == n);
          @ loop_writes i;
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        /*@
          @ // outer loop: rows 0..i-1 of ret have been filled correctly
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @ maintaining (\forall int p; 0 <= p && p < i;
          @                 \forall int q; 0 <= q && q < n;
          @                   ret[p][q] == A[m-1-p][n-1-q]);
          @ loop_writes ret[*], i;
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            /*@
              @ // inner loop: columns 0..j-1 of ret[i] have been filled correctly;
              @ // previous rows remain correct
              @ maintaining 0 <= j && j <= n;
              @ maintaining (\forall int p; 0 <= p && p < i;
              @                 \forall int q; 0 <= q && q < n;
              @                   ret[p][q] == A[m-1-p][n-1-q]);
              @ maintaining (\forall int q; 0 <= q && q < j;
              @                 ret[i][q] == A[m-1-i][n-1-q]);
              @ loop_writes ret[i][*], j;
              @ decreases n - j;
              @*/
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;

    }

    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class RotateMatrix180 {
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;

    }

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class RotateMatrix180 {
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

            return -1;

        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; i++)
            if (arr[i] == elem)
                return i;
        return -1;
    }
}

Please generate JML specifications for the Java program given below.
```
public class RotateMatrix180 {
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}

      @                A[i][j] == \old(A[i][j]));
      @ 
      @ // Behaviors cover all cases and are disjoint
      @ complete behaviors;
      @ disjoint behaviors;

    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {

        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;

        int n = A[0].length;

          @ // check remaining rows are non-null and of length n
          @ maintaining 1 <= i && i <= m;

          @ maintaining (\forall int k; 1 <= k && k < i; A[k] != null && A[k].length == n);
          @ loop_writes i;
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/

            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }

        /*@
          @ // outer loop: rows 0..i-1 of ret have been filled correctly
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= m;

          @                 \forall int q; 0 <= q && q < n;
          @                   ret[p][q] == A[m-1-p][n-1-q]);

          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/

            /*@
              @ // inner loop: columns 0..j-1 of ret[i] have been filled correctly;
              @ // previous rows remain correct
              @ maintaining 0 <= j && j <= n;

              @ loop_writes ret[i][*], j;
              @ decreases n - j;
              @*/
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class RotateMatrix180 {
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}

      @              \forall int j; 0 <= j && j < A[0].length;
      @                A[i][j] == \old(A[i][j]));
      @ 
      @ // Behaviors cover all cases and are disjoint
      @ complete behaviors;
      @ disjoint behaviors;

    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {

        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;

        int n = A[0].length;

          @ // check remaining rows are non-null and of length n
          @ maintaining 1 <= i && i <= m;

          @ loop_writes i;
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/

            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }

        /*@
          @ // outer loop: rows 0..i-1 of ret have been filled correctly
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @ maintaining (\forall int p; 0 <= p && p < i;
          @                 \forall int q; 0 <= q && q < n;
          @                   ret[p][q] == A[m-1-p][n-1-q]);
          @ loop_writes ret[*], i;
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            /*@
              @ // inner loop: columns 0..j-1 of ret[i] have been filled correctly;
              @ // previous rows remain correct
              @ maintaining 0 <= j && j <= n;
              @ maintaining (\forall int p; 0 <= p && p < i;

              @                 ret[i][q] == A[m-1-i][n-1-q]);
              @ loop_writes ret[i][*], j;
              @ decreases n - j;
              @*/
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```

public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```
public class Find {
    public static int find(int[] arr, int elem) {
        if (arr == null)
            return -1;

        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; i++)
            if (arr[i] == elem)
                return i;
        return -1;
    }
}

public class Find {
    //@  requires arr != null;
    //@  requires \exists int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] == elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures 0 <= \result < arr.length;

Please generate JML specifications for the Java program given below.
```
public class RotateMatrix180 {
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}

```

- Do NOT use assumptions ("assume" clause).

- If the method returns a newly allocated object, state the freshness with "ensures \fresh(\result);".

public class RotateMatrix180 {
    /*@ 
      @ // Behavior: A is null or empty -> return null
      @ behavior null_input:
      @   requires A == null || A.length == 0;
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result == null;
      @ 
      @ // Behavior: first row is null or empty -> return null
      @ also
      @ behavior bad_first_row:
      @   requires A != null && A.length > 0 && (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0);
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result == null;
      @ 
      @ // Behavior: some row (1..m-1) is null or has different length -> return null
      @ also
      @ behavior ragged_or_null_row:
      @   requires A != null && A.length > 0 && A[0] != null && A[0].length > 0
      @            && (\exists int k; 1 <= k && k < A.length; A[k] == null || A[k].length != A[0].length);
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result == null;
      @ 
      @ // Behavior: well-formed rectangular non-empty matrix -> return fresh rotated matrix
      @ also
      @ behavior ok:
      @   requires A != null && A.length > 0 && A[0] != null && A[0].length > 0
      @            && (\forall int k; 1 <= k && k < A.length; A[k] != null && A[k].length == A[0].length);
      @   // method allocates and writes the returned matrix and its subarrays
      @   assigns \result, \result[*], \result[*][*];
      @   ensures \result != null;
      @   ensures \fresh(\result);
      @   // dimensions preserved
      @   ensures \result.length == A.length;
      @   ensures (\forall int r; 0 <= r && r < \result.length; \result[r] != null && \result[r].length == A[0].length);
      @   // returned matrix is the 180-degree rotation of A
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length;
      @              \forall int j; 0 <= j && j < A[0].length;
      @                \result[i][j] == A[A.length-1-i][A[0].length-1-j]);
      @   // original matrix not modified
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length;
      @              \forall int j; 0 <= j && j < A[0].length;
      @                A[i][j] == \old(A[i][j]));
      @ 
      @ // Behaviors cover all cases and are disjoint
      @ complete behaviors;
      @ disjoint behaviors;
      @*/
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        /*@ 
          @ // check remaining rows are non-null and of length n
          @ maintaining 1 <= i && i <= m;
          @ maintaining m == A.length && n == A[0].length;
          @ maintaining (\forall int k; 1 <= k && k < i; A[k] != null && A[k].length == n);
          @ loop_writes i;
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        /*@
          @ // outer loop: rows 0..i-1 of ret have been filled correctly
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @ maintaining (\forall int p; 0 <= p && p < i;
          @                 \forall int q; 0 <= q && q < n;
          @                   ret[p][q] == A[m-1-p][n-1-q]);
          @ loop_writes ret[*], i;
          @ decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            /*@
              @ // inner loop: columns 0..j-1 of ret[i] have been filled correctly;
              @ // previous rows remain correct
              @ maintaining 0 <= j && j <= n;
              @ maintaining (\forall int p; 0 <= p && p < i;
              @                 \forall int q; 0 <= q && q < n;
              @                   ret[p][q] == A[m-1-p][n-1-q]);
              @ maintaining (\forall int q; 0 <= q && q < j;
              @                 ret[i][q] == A[m-1-i][n-1-q]);
              @ loop_writes ret[i][*], j;
              @ decreases n - j;
              @*/
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}
